- **cfn-megaplan**: Tiered planning orchestrator. Runs the full SPARC+ pipeline (research, spec, decide, pseudo, data, arch, ux, design, test, ops) as a parallel DAG, scaled by build stage (mvp/beta/enterprise) via inclusion profiles. Enforces two gates: every success criterion is executable (verifiable-done) and every step is unambiguous (haiku-executable). Plain plan mode is the default for feature work; use this only for enterprise, compliance, ops-heavy or migration-rehearsal builds (mvp/beta programs: cfn-megaplan-fast). ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cfn-megaplan) · ★13)
- **cfn-megaplan-fast**: Token-lean planning orchestrator for multi-part programs and single features. Program mode runs spec/data/arch/ux ONCE, then only test_plan + write_plan + Bar A per part, reading section extracts not whole artifacts. Hard byte caps per artifact, static-only bars (1 round, inline patch), opus only for spec+arch, no nested subagents. Same hand-off contract to /cfn-loop-task as megaplan. Use for mvp/beta work; full /cfn-megaplan stays for enterprise/compliance/ops-heavy builds. ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cfn-megaplan-fast) · ★13)
- **cfn-megaplan-lite**: Balanced-cut planning mode for medium features (3-7 files, single shared-state surface). Runs a 7-level DAG at mvp depth but roughly half the wall-clock of full megaplan: both completion bars capped to 1 round, pseudo folded into arch, non-core phases on sonnet, and the live haiku probe dropped. Inherits megaplan's bar scripts and phase skills by relative path. Use as the balanced-cut alternative to /cfn-megaplan for medium features. ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cfn-megaplan-lite) · ★13)
- **cfn-memory-management**: Prevent and detect memory leaks in Claude CLI operations through proactive monitoring, limits, and profiling. Use when managing memory limits for long-running agents, detecting memory leaks, profiling heap usage, or implementing recovery procedures. ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/cfn-extras/skills/cfn-memory-persistence/lib/management) · ★13)
- **cfn-memory-monitoring**: **Implementation Date:** 2025-11-04 **Purpose:** Add memory reporting to Docker deployment process to prevent WSL2 crashes ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/cfn-extras/skills/analytics/cfn-memory-monitoring) · ★13)
- **cfn-memory-persistence**: Data persistence for CFN Loop: SQLite storage, Redis coordination, auto memory persistence. Use when storing/retrieving CFN state, coordinating via Redis, or persisting agent memory. ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/cfn-extras/skills/cfn-memory-persistence) · ★13)

- **cfn-migration-rehearsal**: Rehearses a DB migration up+down round-trip against an EXPLICIT scratch database only. Applies up, captures schema, applies down, re-captures, and diffs to prove up and down are inverses. Refuses to run without CFN_SCRATCH_DATABASE_URL and refuses anything that looks like prod. Executes what cfn-ops only designs. ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cfn-migration-rehearsal) · ★13)
- **cfn-monitor**: Post-deploy runtime health gate and incident triage runbook. Probes HTTP endpoints for status and latency, emits JSON summary, exits nonzero on any failure. Use after every deploy as a go/no-go gate. Stateless single-shot; see cfn: marker for the continuous-polling upgrade path. ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/cfn-extras/skills/cfn-monitor) · ★13)
- **cfn-night-mode**: Global autonomy flag with decision log and morning report. While on, sessions never stop on AskUserQuestion or plan approval stalls: they decide with a conservative reversible default, log every choice to decision-log under night-&lt;date&gt; slugs, commit finished work without pushing, and surface the whole batch in a morning report the next day. ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cfn-night-mode) · ★13)

- **cfn-operations**: File and log operations for CFN. Use when reading/writing/managing files or appending/reading/rotating log files in CFN workflows. ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/cfn-extras/skills/cfn-operations) · ★13)
- **cfn-ops**: Operations-design phase. Design the production surface UP FRONT: threat model (STRIDE), observability (logs/metrics/traces), rollout (flags/canary), success metrics/KPIs, failure modes (FMEA), capacity/cost, rollback rehearsal, runbook. Use in cfn-megaplan for beta+ tiers so 'how it runs in prod and how we know it worked' is designed, not review-checkboxed. ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cfn-ops) · ★13)
- **cfn-parameterized-queries**: Parameterized SQL execution, blocks injection. Use when executing DB queries, inserting/updating records, or any SQL op needing security hardening. ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cfn-parameterized-queries) · ★13)

- **cfn-perf-gate**: Performance regression gate. Runs a configurable benchmark command, compares results against a stored baseline, and emits a cfn-vote-implement manifest for every path that regressed beyond CFN_PERF_THRESHOLD_PCT. Set CFN_PERF_BENCH_CMD to a command that outputs {name: number} JSON. Never auto-fixes. ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cfn-perf-gate) · ★13)
- **cfn-persona-verify**: Role-coherence gate. Drives the running app as a named role, using that project's local role-&lt;slug&gt; skills as ground truth, and flags capabilities the role should have but cannot reach (and denied ones it can). Catches the class no mechanical gate catches: correct, coherent, tests green, and nonsense for the person using it. Observe-only by default; writes are opt-in per capability and may only touch rows the pass created. Emits a cfn-vote-implement manifest. Never auto-fixes and never rewrites role docs. ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cfn-persona-verify) · ★13)
- **cfn-plan-review**: Post-planning completeness review. Extracts assumptions, traces dependencies, analyzes blast radius, checks alpha-readiness, surfaces gaps before implementation. Use after writing any plan that touches data, APIs, or shared state. ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cfn-plan-review) · ★13)
- **cfn-planning**: Epic decomposition, coordinator planning, scope management.
